fakat suredeki bütün çabalar bu olumlu türe indirgenmez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B004","root_000709/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_589f74a1d3b8f173394d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:4:2\",\"root_000709\",[\"B004\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Aynı etkinlik alanının uzlaştırıcı cömertliğe de başkasına zarar veren toplumsal girişime de açılabilmesi, ayrışmayı kişisel alışkanlığın ötesine taşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000775/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5ec3c4d6384adbe9a452","text":"3. **Distance between two things.** Evidence: `ش ت ت` B003 (two things far apart), activated by 92:13 “last and first” and the opposed yusr/‘usr pair. Structural cue: the surah repeatedly sets pairs in reverse or contrast. Mechanism: difference becomes increasing distance between beginnings and ends. Abductive move: I infer temporal widening: small orientation choices become far-apart destinies. Reading change: “diverse” becomes a widening separation across time.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B004","root_000709/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8013783bb9bf935e5629","text":"2. **Noble undertaking vs denunciatory agency.** Evidence: `س ع ي` B006 (noble generosity/peacemaking) and B004 (denunciation to authority). Structural cue: giving/guarding/truth in 92:5-7 faces withholding/denial/fall in 92:8-11. Mechanism: the same root of agency can carry reparative generosity or destructive social action. Abductive move: I supply a hidden social arena where actions affect others. Reading change: the ayah reads as a fork in agency, not merely different personal habits.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_86d2684c3bd5e2b13391","text":"92:16 identified the fire-bound person as denial plus turning away, making “الأشقى” a trajectory rather than a label. 92:17’s opposite, being kept aside, also made `ص ل ي` B007 follower imagery more plausible: each person follows a path into contact or avoidance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B001","root_000775/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c84a54f94c00ebef94a1","text":"1. **Endeavor as a branching trajectory.** `سَعْي` anchors `س ع ي:B001` (purposeful movement toward a sought end) and `س ع ي:B002` (work, earning, and active conduct); `شَتَّىٰ` anchors `ش ت ت:B001` (scattering) and `ش ت ت:B003` (distance between alternatives). The paired sequences of giving/guarding/confirming in 92:5–7 and withholding/self-sufficiency/denial in 92:8–9 concretize the initially unspecified divergence. The packet supplies two sequences, while the inference is that repeated acts generate distance between routes. The ayah shifts from “people do different things” to a kinetic model in which conduct progressively separates its agents.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cd9f5d502b3e21158991","text":"1. **Striving as divergent work and earning.** Evidence: `س ع ي` B002 (work/earning/active endeavor), `ش ت ت` B001 (scattering/separation). Structural cue: the ayah summarizes before the two “as for the one…” paths. Mechanism: one field of effort branches into incompatible trajectories. Abductive move: I infer that “scattered” refers to functional outcomes, not random variety. Reading change: “your striving” becomes a labor economy whose returns are split by orientation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B002","root_000709/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d5475a4c8e4cdf0c268d","text":"2. **A noble subset remains visible without exhausting the whole.** `س ع ي:B006` names striving for noble generosity, directly activated by giving in 92:5 and the good/beautiful object in 92:6. Yet `س ع ي:B002` explicitly includes both good and bad work, and `ش ت ت:B001` prevents the generous branch from absorbing every endeavor. The structural cue is asymmetrical specification after a neutral plural address. The reading change is diagnostic: noble generosity is one sharply visible realization of striving, while the fixed ayah still holds multiple, incompatible realizations open.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B006","root_000775/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d9a22ebdde38b817959e","text":"92:5–9 transformed `ش ت ت:B001/B003` from miscellaneous difference into accumulating distance between two sequenced courses. Giving in 92:5 was the later surprise that activated `س ع ي:B006` (noble generosity), while 92:8–9 corrected that branch from becoming an exhaustive gloss of all striving.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
